Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in symplectic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 147 and the invariant I(A,B) = 822 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 147 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the symplectic structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 822. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
